# 当前状态——权威快照（2026-11-09）

## 2026-11-09 单步扩散超分与后验 Flow 新先例复核

- 已核对 CODSR 的 CVPR 2026 官方记录与作者全文，以及 AISTATS 2026 条件 Flow Matching 后验论文的 PMLR 记录和 OpenReview 全文。两者分别收窄自然图像单步扩散/区域先验与通用后验 Flow 的宽泛新颖性，不涉及 SAR 联合去斑超分、匹配 optical 增益或本地观测模型。详见[中文审查报告](../../experiments/literature_audits/flow_and_one_step_sr_prior_art_20261109_v1/审查报告.md)。
- 未访问项目数据、模型、checkpoint 或 confirmation/test 载荷；未训练、未推理、未使用 GPU。H1–H6、C2/C3/C4 gate、C3-A v3、数据权限和训练暂停均不变。
- **下一步：** 继续只做新的一手来源、train/development 专属元数据或有直接源码/冻结协议依据的 CPU 工作；本地模型效果仍等待训练恢复和合规权重。

## 2026-11-09 已知后验评分诊断与 Flow 逆问题文献补查

- 核对 ICLR 2026 的 DPS 统计基准全文与 ICML 2026 MPC-Flow 出版方摘要。前者以精确 Lévy 后验样本作分布级对照；后者扩大了通用 Flow 逆问题推理控制的先例范围。MPC-Flow 本轮仅摘要级证据，未取得 PDF 正文；两者均不提供本地 SAR、光学引导或本地表示域似然证据。审查记录：[文献报告](../../experiments/literature_audits/posterior_sampling_statistical_benchmark_20261109_v1/报告.md)。
- 完成六维稀疏高斯混合线性逆问题的纯 NumPy CPU 评分校准诊断：1,024 个案例、64 个精确后验成分。精确后验平均 CRPS 为 0.120799，欠分散/过分散/均值塌缩条件分别为 0.136522/0.131598/0.177281；精确后验 50%/90% 区间覆盖为 0.4990/0.9014，全部预声明判据通过。完整结果见[报告](../../experiments/synthetic_cpu/posterior_score_calibration_cpu_20261109_v1r1/报告.md)。
- 首轮实现误用 5 维而非协议规定的 6 维，已标为无效并保留全部产物；新增维度断言按预期发现问题，修正后在唯一 v1r1 目录按原参数和判据复跑，没有覆盖首轮结果或改门槛。
- 仅读取公开文献与本轮合成产物；项目图像、数据清单、模型/checkpoint、confirmation/test 载荷及测量值均未访问，未训练、未推理、未用 GPU。C1/C2/C3 状态、C3-A v3、数据权限和 gate 均不变。运行前分区约 19.2 GB 可用（96% 使用）；本轮仅生成小型 CSV/JSON/Markdown 产物，专用 `/tmp` 编译缓存已清理。
- **下一步：** 训练继续按用户要求暂停；仅处理新的一手文献、可核验 train/development 元数据或有直接源码/冻结协议证据的非 GPU 问题。未取得新来源时，不重试 MPC-Flow PDF 端点；真实模型分布评价仍须等训练恢复并另行冻结协议。

## 2026-11-09 局部亮点护栏阈值敏感性解析诊断

- 对背景均值 0.04、目标均值 0.12/0.20/0.30/0.45、Gamma 形状 4/16/64、9 个阈值进行 108 组解析计算。固定阈值 0.25 时，单 draw 目标命中率从 8.72×10⁻¹² 到 0.999989；在独立像素假设下，整图 16 draws 至少一次背景误报概率按 Gamma 形状分别为 0.002674、2.19×10⁻²¹、6.39×10⁻⁹³。
- 这说明 0.25 的命中/误报权衡对目标均值和乘性分布参数敏感；图像级误报概率还依赖像素独立假设，不能迁移至真实 SAR。没有据此选阈值或修改评价器、C3-A v3、阶段 gate、训练状态或数据权限。详见[解析诊断报告](../../experiments/synthetic_cpu/c3a_bright_scatterer_threshold_sensitivity_cpu_20261109_v1/报告.md)。
- **下一步：** 训练/GPU 继续暂停；局部亮点量仍是未启用的候选评价护栏，任何真实输出应用都须另行冻结阈值来源、目标/背景定义和空间相关处理。

## 2026-11-09 稀疏亮点与图幅敏感性 CPU 诊断

- 两项预登记合成诊断显示：64×64、4 个亮点的五种预测条件下，逐像素 CRPS、整图能量和邻接变差均可区分错误条件；压暗条件的配对正差比例为 0.893。局部精确检出、3×3 邻域检出和伪亮点率补充了不同错误类型的信息。
- 单亮点漏检实验将图幅从 64×64 增至 256×256，亮点密度降低 16 倍；三项全图分数的绝对配对差约缩小 15–16 倍，但本次共享噪声配对下，各尺寸的正差比例仍为 0.970–0.993。该比例不是模型检出概率，跨图幅绝对分差不宜直接比较。
- 这些结果只适用于预设归一化合成场、Gamma 随机数与阈值；不代表真实 SAR、模型预测或干净参考恢复质量。评分公式、C3-A v3、阶段 gate、训练状态和数据权限均不变。详细记录见[评分敏感性报告](../../experiments/synthetic_cpu/c3a_bright_scatterer_metric_sensitivity_cpu_20261109_v1/报告.md)及[图幅敏感性报告](../../experiments/synthetic_cpu/c3a_bright_scatterer_resolution_sweep_cpu_20261109_v1/报告.md)。
- `related_work_matrix.csv` 的中文说明列已统一为简体中文，题名、作者、刊物、DOI、链接及标准术语保留原文；记录只描述文档整理，不增加文献证据。
- **下一步：** 训练/GPU 继续暂停；局部亮点统计只保留为补充设计候选。真实预测分布的评分适用性与亮点护栏阈值仍未知，不接入正式评价器或 gate。

## 2026-11-09 横纵相关轴交换 CPU 诊断

- 在 24×24 logistic-normal 可分离 AR(1) 场中交换横纵相关值 0.75/0.35，按预登记协议每个真值方向生成 300 个目标、每目标 16 draws。
- 两个真值方向的 h=1 与四尺度变差分数排序率均为 1.000，且平均分差 95% 区间下界均大于零；CRPS/整图能量排序接近 0.5。h=8 单尺度排序率为 0.593。
- 现有评分逐边计算平方误差，在此构造中保留了方向差异，暂未发现支持改代码的证据。h=8 区分较弱；距离效用不均不代表可据单一案例调整权重。
- 仅 NumPy CPU；独立核验 4,200 行 CSV、汇总和四项哈希。无项目数据、模型/checkpoint、confirmation/test、PyTorch、训练、推理或 GPU 访问。详见[实验报告](../../experiments/synthetic_cpu/c3a_spatial_variogram_anisotropy_cpu_20261109_v1/report.md)。

## 2026-11-09 多尺度变差分数相关强度敏感性 CPU 诊断

- 按预登记协议将一阶高斯相关从 0.8 扩展到 ρ={0.4,0.6,0.8,0.9}，比较 AR(1)/RBF 两种真值方向；每条件 300 个目标、每目标 16 draws。
- 8 个条件中 7 个同时满足多尺度平均分差 95% 区间下界大于零且正确排序比例高于 0.65。唯一未达到者为 ρ=0.4、RBF 真值，排序比例 0.600；其平均差区间仍大于零。全部 h=1 排序比例为 0.463–0.550。
- 这说明多尺度区分受相关强度与真值方向影响。结果只适用于指定的 24×24 logistic-normal AR(1)/RBF 合成场；仅支持将候选保留待进一步验证，不推广到真实 SAR、不更改 C3-A v3、评价器或任何 gate。
- CSV、汇总和四项产物哈希独立复核通过。仅 NumPy CPU；无 PyTorch、训练、推理、GPU、项目载荷、模型/checkpoint、confirmation/test 或测试测量值访问。详见[实验报告](../../experiments/synthetic_cpu/c3a_spatial_variogram_correlation_sweep_cpu_20261109_v1/report.md)。

## 2026-11-09 多尺度变差分数的远距离依赖 CPU 诊断

- 按[预登记协议](../../experiments/synthetic_cpu/c3a_spatial_variogram_multiscale_cpu_20261109_v1/protocol.md)，比较一阶相关匹配为 0.8、边际相同而远距离相关不同的 AR(1)/RBF logistic-normal 场。两种真值方向各 300 个目标，每个预测集合 16 draws。
- 单尺度 `h=1` 变差分数排序比例为 0.490/0.473；四尺度 `h={1,2,4,8}` 等权分数为 0.990/0.857，两个方向平均配对分差的 95% 区间均大于零。结果符合预登记的合成诊断判据。
- 结论只适用于该尺寸、两个可分离协方差核及有限 draw 数；不表示真实 SAR/模型分布结论，不改变 C3-A v3 或 gate。未访问项目载荷、模型/checkpoint、confirmation/test，无 GPU。
- 记录：[实验报告](../../experiments/synthetic_cpu/c3a_spatial_variogram_multiscale_cpu_20261109_v1/report.md)。

## 2026-11-09 PCSDD 复数 SAR 扩散去斑全文审查

- 审阅作者公开的 CC BY 4.0 全文，并以 IEEE 官方 DOI 记录核对论文身份。PCSDD 在复数 SLC 上结合各向异性 PDE 漂移与学习型 score 修正，使用 blind-spot 自监督、强度域 Gamma 似然及 ENL/边缘相关约束；实验报告 Gaofen-3 C 波段 SLC、1 m 分辨率。
- 这是复数 SAR 去斑的相邻先例，不涉及超分观测算子、光学引导或错配回退。其表示域不同于本地 normalized 8-bit PNG/noisy pseudo-target，不能直接作为本地物理模型。正文对 blind-spot 掩码 `M` 的符号约定内部不一致，不能独立确认无中心像素泄漏声明；不据此推断实际代码发生泄漏。正式 IEEE 全文页面自动访问受限；作者公开全文的版本上传关系、代码和场景级 split 细节未核实。
- 不改变研究总纲 v1.8、C2/C3/C4 gate、C3-A v3、训练暂停状态或数据权限。没有运行代码、读取项目载荷或使用 GPU。
- 记录：[全文审查报告](../../experiments/literature_audits/pcsdd_complex_diffusion_despeckling_fulltext_20261109_v1/report.md)。

## 2026-11-09 多样本分布评价、图像空间依赖与样本数纯 CPU 诊断

- 在 v1.8 生效后，完成三项预登记的纯合成 CPU 实验。二维双峰已知分布实验显示：均值塌缩点的总体均值误差为 0，但能量分数为 3.0910、成对距离与模态支持率均为 0；真实抽样能量分数为 1.8094、支持率为 0.9704。矩匹配单峰高斯能量分数为 1.9378，模态支持率为 0.5072。样本数实验在 100 次重复中比较 N=4–256：能量分数正确排序比例达到预登记 0.90 描述阈值时，矩匹配高斯为 N=32、0.8/0.2 偏置双峰为 N=16。32×32 归一化图像域案例中，同边缘但空间依赖错误的 Gamma 乘性样本，其逐像素 CRPS 配对差为 −0.0000128±0.0001670，而整图能量与邻接变差分数分别高 0.0051899 和 0.0044720。
- 解释仅限指定合成分布、样本数和评价量。结果说明点估计、分布形状、模态质量与集合大小需要分开报告；不构成真实 Flow/Diffusion 校准、SAR 物理一致性、C3-A 阶段证据或模型 draw 数建议。
- C3-A v3、训练配置、C2/C3 gate 和所有阶段资格均未改变。训练/GPU 继续暂停；没有访问项目影像、模型、checkpoint、confirmation/test 载荷或测试测量值。
- 逐重复结果与运行清单的 SHA-256 核验通过；本轮新增合成产物共 284,563 字节，清理了本轮生成的 3 个 Python 编译缓存，没有删除数据、历史结果或 checkpoint。最新空间读数为 21,044,301,824 字节可用（19.60 GiB，已用 90.7%）。
- 记录：[分布评价量诊断](../../experiments/synthetic_cpu/c3a_distribution_metric_sanity_cpu_20261109_v1/report.md)、[样本数敏感性诊断](../../experiments/synthetic_cpu/c3a_distribution_draw_count_cpu_20261109_v1/report.md)、[图像空间依赖指标诊断](../../experiments/synthetic_cpu/c3a_image_distribution_metrics_cpu_20261109_v1/report.md)。
- **设计产物：** 已形成[C3-A 多样本分布评价补充设计](C3A_DISTRIBUTION_EVALUATION_ADDENDUM_DESIGN_20261109.md)，明确候选分数、数据边界和恢复训练后需冻结的项目。该设计稿不启用新协议。
- **文献核验：** 已读取 CRPS、能量分数及变差分数的两篇一手来源全文，确认能量分数对部分相关失配可能不够敏感，变差分数虽适当但非严格适当；相应局限已写入设计、综述与风险记录。来源审查：[报告](../../experiments/literature_audits/proper_scoring_rules_multisample_eval_20261109_v1/report.md)。
- **下一步：** 训练仍暂停；继续寻找有直接源码/协议依据的非 GPU 工作。真实生成分布评价要等用户恢复训练并另行冻结补充评价协议后进行。

## C3-A 多样本评价器与冻结协议相符 — 2026-11-07

- 只读核对 protocol v3 与 evaluator：每个 draw 独立算标量指标，再汇总逐身份指标和逐像素标准差；不计算均值图质量、分布距离或 draw 相关系数，且输出不保留预测图。该范围符合冻结协议，不是实现缺陷。
- 四个 draw 的标准差只能描述当前设置下的采样变化；不能证明 draw 独立或分布已校准。未来如增加均值图/分布/依赖评价，须另行预登记，不能回写 C3-A v3。

## 暂停训练期间的双峰流 CPU 方法探索 — 2026-11-07

- 用解析双峰条件流完成等速度场调用数求解器压力诊断、分离度扫描和时间网格比较；见[主报告](../../experiments/synthetic_cpu/c3a_bimodal_flow_solver_spike_20261107_v1/report.md)、[分离度补充](../../experiments/synthetic_cpu/c3a_bimodal_separation_sweep_cpu_20261107_v1/report.md)与[网格补充](../../experiments/synthetic_cpu/c3a_bimodal_time_grid_cpu_20261107_v1/report.md)。均为合成 Level 0 结果，不形成模型质量、物理一致性或晋级证据。简单非均匀网格未改善强分离解析场。
- 又在 `[0,1]` 表示尺度和 `noise_sigma=0.1` 下完成标量双峰核验；Heun 8 步中央窗质量约为理论值 2.17 倍，Heun 32 步接近理论/独立样本参考。用同一速度场经仓库 `sample_heun` CPU 运行，输出与独立 NumPy 更新最大绝对差 `2.220e-16`；采样器定向测试 5 项通过。见[表示尺度与实现核验报告](../../experiments/synthetic_cpu/c3a_representation_scale_bimodal_cpu_20261107_v1/report.md)。
- 主压力、分离度扫描、时间网格和表示尺度脚本分别通过 30、23、22、16 条断言；7 个本轮 Python 脚本语法检查通过，52 个记录文件未发现行尾空格；各实验清单输入/产物摘要均匹配。未访问项目影像、模型权重、confirmation/test 载荷或历史结果；CUDA 设备不可见，没有训练。
- 这些解析压力测试不改变冻结的均匀 Heun 8 步、输出裁剪、训练协议或任何阶段 gate。C3-A 训练按用户要求暂停；继续做静态代码、协议和可独立验证的 CPU 工作。2026-11-07 最后复核时项目分区可用 17,952,563,200 字节（约 16.72 GiB，97%）；没有删除文件。项目整体研究目标未完成，当前不提交/推送。

## 无 GPU 输出裁剪边界偏差诊断 — 2026-11-07 14:24（中国标准时间）

- 完成 21 种目标亮度×噪声条件的纯 NumPy CPU 合成对照，每种噪声生成 500,000 个样本，共 45 次断言。对 `[0,1]` 内真值，裁剪逐样本不增加平方误差。

## 无 GPU 求解器计算预算比较与 v1.7 数据边界 — 2026-11-07 13:27（中国标准时间）

- 完成纯 NumPy CPU 解析高斯流对照：Euler 16 步、Heun 8 步、显式中点法 8 步、经典 RK4 4 步，均为每样本 16 次速度场调用；三个目标/源标准差比共 12 组。中点法在比值 0.25 时误差最低；RK4 在比值 1 和 2 时最低；Euler 在三种比值下误差最大。Heun 结果与此前独立步数实验逐条件完全一致。
- **解释范围：** 仅说明在已知解析的一维高斯速度场上，等 RHS 调用预算下求解器排序会随方差比而变；不代表神经速度场、SAR 图像质量或实际 GPU 延迟。冻结 C3-A 的 Heun 8 步设置、协议与 gate 均不变。

- **纯合成观测实验：** 固定 `L=8`，对 `Y=sN`、`s∈{0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3,1}`，仅用五档等权训练混合拟合一个共享 `lambda=1.2217133`，再以独立校准集和五组留出样本比较。留出 `ΔD=D(log-YJ)−D(log-only)` 的五档等权均值为 `−0.00634053`，每档均为 5/5 次负差；改善幅度随信号强度变化。详见[协议与报告](../../experiments/synthetic_cpu/log_yj_multiplicative_observation_levels_20261107_v1/report.md)。
- 该对照只说明固定 Gamma 模型与合成信号网格下的条件分布形状；不代表真实 SAR 或项目表示域。结合前一项 Gamma 乘子实验，只能说 log-YJ 在这两种合成设置中降低经验 KS 距离，不能据此移植到 C3-A。
- **代码静态审查：** C3-A 训练入口、目标集读取、C2 固定输入生成器与退化预处理在当前核验范围内符合冻结的数据域/变换合同；训练入口包含 loss、梯度与参数有限性检查。没有找到足以支持代码修改的缺口，本轮未改模型或训练器。
- **C1 元数据：** SEN12MS 作者相关维护仓库的 README 指向 ROI 地图 Notebook，但目前没有可确认的 train/development 专属坐标交叉表。仅查入口/说明/目录名；未开 split 清单内容、Notebook 输出、GeoTIFF 或任何影像。详见[路线审查](../../experiments/data_audits/sen12ms_author_repo_crosswalk_route_20261107_v1/report.md)。地理/采集/footprint 独立性仍未知。
- 用户要求的训练暂停继续生效；本轮无 GPU、训练、模型评价、项目测试或项目数据载荷访问。C2 gate、C3-A 结果和阶段资格未改变。

## log-Yeo–Johnson Gamma 变换纯合成 CPU 对照完成；训练继续暂停 — 2026-11-07 08:51（中国标准时间）

- 完成独立拟合/评估的纯合成对照：`N ~ Gamma(L, scale=1/L)`，`L=1,2,4,8`；每个 L 使用 100,000 个拟合样本及 5 组各 100,000 个独立评估样本，Yeo–Johnson 的 `lambda` 仅由拟合样本确定。
- 20 组配对评估中，log-Yeo–Johnson 相对 log-only 的标准正态 KS 距离差 `ΔD` 等权平均为 `−0.040896`；四个 L 的平均差均为负，且各自 5/5 次重复均为负。逐组结果、偏度、超额峰度及 Gamma 样本矩见[实验报告](../../experiments/synthetic_cpu/log_yj_gamma_normality_20261107_v1/report.md)。
- 该观察仅说明所设定的抽象 Gamma 边际分布在本模拟中更接近标准正态；它不是 SAR 物理模型、项目数据、C3-A 质量或变换迁移效果证据。没有将其移入项目代码或冻结协议。

## DiffusionSAR 一手全文审查收窄宽泛去斑主张；训练继续暂停 — 2026-11-07 08:29（中国标准时间）

- University of Twente 仓储中的 2024 TGRS 期刊最终版全文已核实：DiffusionSAR 是以带斑 SAR 观测为条件的 DDPM 去斑；合成训练从 Sentinel-2 参考图生成 Gamma speckle，并含 log-Yeo–Johnson 变换与多时相 SAR 微调路径。它不做 SAR 超分，也没有 inference-time optical 条件。
- 该工作收窄“条件扩散 SAR 去斑”“Gamma speckle 扩散”宽泛新颖性表述，但不验证本地联合去斑/超分、optical 增益、失配回退或 C1 8-bit 表示域。该全文还存在 real data 被称作 single-look intensity 与 SLC 的术语不一致；本项目不推断其产品类型。
- 审查报告、manifest、相关工作矩阵及研究记录已更新，详见[全文审查](../../experiments/literature_audits/diffusionsar_conditional_sar_despeckling_fulltext_20261107_v1/report.md)。没有移植该论文变换，没有改模型、代码、协议或 gate。
- 本轮未读项目图像、checkpoint、预测、指标或 confirmation/test 载荷；未训练、未调用 GPU、未评价模型。此前终端显示历史 confirmation 汇总文字的事件仍按 D-2026-11-07-025 隔离。

- **诊断协议与结果：** 按独立冻结的 [v4 协议](../../protocols/mmcanet_phase_c2/c2_zero_background_contrast_cpu_20261107_v4.json)，只分析原 C2 v2 fail-closed 记录中的 6 条“条件×样本”development 案例，来自 2 个来源组。仅使用 4 个控制（matched/permuted/missing/SAR-only），严格加载 8 个已有 best checkpoint，并在 CPU 上完成 24 次前向；实际仅读取这 6 条记录及冻结置换 donor 所需的 24 个 development 数组。报告、逐样本结果、配对差和 manifest 见[运行目录](../../experiments/diagnostics/c2_zero_background_contrast_cpu_20261107_v4/)。
- **结果边界：** 所有案例的目标背景中位数 TBR 均保持 `null`，状态为 `undefined_target_zero_background_median`；未加 epsilon。matched−SAR-only 的均值/中位数对比绝对误差差值分别为：x2_light −0.000569/−0.001011、x2_severe −0.006447/−0.007495、x4_light −0.003836/+0.003685、x4_severe +0.008148/−0.005016。负值仅表示该选中样本的候选误差较低；每条件只有 1–2 条且总计 2 个来源组、来自既有指标失败选样，不能解释为总体效用或泛化证据。C2 gate 仍未判定，H1/Q1 不更新。
- **实现与验证：** `C2MatchedJoint` 仅移除 CUDA-only 前置检查，网络计算、参数、state dict 和 GPU 路径未改；新增两个倍率的 CPU 前向和缺失 optical 合成测试。C2 CPU runner 边界测试与模型 CPU 测试共 10 项通过，相关文件 `py_compile` 通过。v4 manifest 记录 24 次前向全部成功、8 个 checkpoint 身份、24 个数组 SHA-256，且协议/模型/runner 哈希复核一致。按用户暂停要求未运行 GPU 回归。

## 无 GPU 表示域范围审计与裁剪分解完成；训练继续暂停 — 2026-11-07 02:13（中国标准时间）

- **数值域审计：** 按冻结 C3-A `x2_light` 输入，在 CPU 上核验 train 1,600 个身份/92 个来源组和 development 200 个身份/19 个来源组，仅读取各自 `lr` 与 `target` 数组并逐文件校验 SHA-256。来源组等权统计显示 SAR LR 中 21.57%/22.16% 像素大于 1；按模型实际 `bilinear(align_corners=false)` 上采样后，条件中 14.94%/15.29% 像素大于 1。noisy pseudo-target 中约 1.23%/1.28% 像素恰为 1，约 1.73%/1.72% 大于等于 0.99。目标与条件的逐样本 MAE 来源组均值约 0.1451/0.1454。完整逐身份数值见[审计报告](../../experiments/diagnostics/c3a_representation_range_audit_20261107_v1/report.md)与 `per_sample.csv`。
- **解释边界：** 以上是归一化 8-bit PNG 派生表示域统计，不代表定标幅度/强度/功率、真实散射体饱和或物理散斑性质。它说明未来总裁剪比例需要区分下界与上界越界，但不支持立即改变模型、损失、裁剪规则或任何 gate。
- **方法/代码改进：** 保留尚未运行的多次采样协议 v2，新增 [v3 协议](../../protocols/mmcanet_phase_c3a/multi_sample_stability_c1_v2_20261105_v3.json)：在原 PSNR/SSIM/EPI、采样和选模规则不变的前提下，记录 raw 输出低于 0 和高于 1 的比例，并按来源组汇总。代码校验两比例之和等于原总裁剪比例；v3 SHA-256 为 `ee83e2cb4dadea7d4fca6576e479d2922d70df44935f1be51116add26e06016c`。v2 原件保留，v2/v3 结果目录均不存在。

- **资源：** 范围审计开始/结束分区可用空间分别为 25,526,722,560 / 24,977,276,928 字节；本轮没有清理数据或历史产物，仅新增轻量 CSV/JSON/Markdown 与脚本。

本次审阅只查阅了含历史 test 关联说明的项目文字记录；本任务 `test_set_accessed=true`（文字记录）、`test_set_used=false`。没有打开 test 图像、测量数组、test 指标文件或 checkpoint，也没有读取 confirmation 载荷。该文字暴露不改变现有历史标记或任何模型选择。

## 当前阶段快照 — 总纲 v1.6 已采纳

- **正式方向：** 以[研究总纲 v1.6](RESEARCH_CHARTER.md)为准；六个科学问题和 C1–C6 路线已采纳，不启动新总纲审批。
- **C1 划分：** 直接使用项目冻结的 C1 v2 来源场景文件夹划分，没有重新随机切分：train 1,600/92 组、development 200/19 组、confirmation 200/20 组。train/development 已用于既有 C2 v2 训练和选择；confirmation 已按原身份清单取得并核验。来源组仅证明文件夹级分离，地理/采集独立性及精细空间重叠仍未知。
- **C2 v2 原 gate：** 四条件八组训练全部完成；正式 development paired screen 因零背景 median TBR 按冻结规则 fail-closed，原 gate 仍为“未判定”。该结果、checkpoint 与历史 manifest 均未改写。
- **C1 v2 confirmation 描述性复核：** 复用冻结八个 best checkpoint，在四种退化条件和四种推理控制下覆盖 200 个 confirmation 样本、20 个来源组。恢复运行状态为 `complete`、预登记状态为 `inconclusive`：matched−SAR-only PSNR 为 −0.0114、−0.0108、+0.0048、−0.0098 dB；matched−permuted 为 +0.0035 至 +0.0047 dB。除 x2_light 的 matched−SAR-only 区间外，其余区间跨零；没有条件达到 +0.05 dB 阈值。结果只属单 seed、来源文件夹划分和表示域 noisy pseudo-target 的描述性观察，不晋级 B1，也不替代 C2 v2 原 gate。
- **协议偏差：** confirmation 载荷访问后发现两处执行器字段映射错误。第一次在固定数组校验后、模型推理前失败；第二次完成四条件推理并在内存计算 3,200 行指标，但在结果落盘前失败，指标未查看、未用于模型选择。修复后使用同一数据、checkpoint、指标和阈值做技术恢复运行；该结果明确标为访问后修订的非晋级复核。三份协议记录、两份失败执行记录和恢复运行均保留，见下方本轮记录。
- **访问标记：** 本轮 `confirmation_payload_accessed=true`、`test_set_accessed=false`、`test_set_used=false`。项目累计 test 标记仍为 `true/true`，源于旧本地训练误用旧 `test_100` 的 13 个样本；该历史污染与 C1 v2 confirmation 无关。确认集今后不得用于调参、筛模型或重复作选择。
- **历史门槛：** B1/B1R/B1F 仍未晋级，B1 为 `not_promoted / stop_at_b1`；B2/B3 继续关闭。总纲采纳、执行修正和本次描述性结果均不更改历史门槛。
- **下一步：** C1 v2 confirmation 已封存。C3-A SAR-only Flow Matching 筛选协议、代码和 CPU 验收已准备；首次 GPU 检查空闲显存为 1.06/3.67 GiB，最近四次提权预检发现 3 个其他 CUDA 计算进程。最新 `AISAR` 直接入口预检仍以退出码 2 在读图前停止；详见[最新预检记录](../../experiments/diagnostics/c3a_gpu_preflight_20261105_v6/manifest.json)。不再重复查询未变化的状态；待 GPU 进程退出并通过新鲜显存预检后，直接使用既有 C1 v2 `x2_light` train/development 固定输入运行；不重新划分，也不访问 confirmation/test。

## C3-A SAR-only 条件流匹配筛选准备 — 2026-11-05（GPU 训练待资源）

按冻结 C1 v2 来源场景文件夹划分开展单因素探索：使用 `data/derived/c2_fixed_inputs_c1_v2_20261031_v1/x2_light/train` 的 1,600 个样本/92 个来源组训练，使用对应 development 的 200 个样本/19 个来源组逐轮评价；与同一固定输入上的既有 C2 `sar_only` 最佳 checkpoint 配对比较。协议为[预登记文件](../../protocols/mmcanet_phase_c3a/sar_only_flow_c1_v2_20261105_v1.json)，SHA-256 为 `147c361b3e91bc4cd8d76ce5c464ad9d2137f2d06b38f31a0bc7c7a7e93101a7`，没有重新切分。`data/` 下数字命名的 2,000 对原始 PNG 与 C1 身份尚无映射，本实验不使用该子集。

新增[模型与数据通路](../../MMCANetV2/phase_c3a_flow.py)及[训练入口](../../scripts/run_phase_c3a_sar_flow.py)：像素域 SAR-only conditional rectified flow，source 为双线性 SAR 上采样加 `0.1 * N(0,I)`，优化 velocity MSE，固定每个 `sample_id` 的噪声，用 8 步 Heun 采样；5 轮、batch=4、单 seed。代码仅读固定输入中的 SAR LR 与 noisy pseudo-target，不加载 optical 数组；开发集只用于选最佳轮次和探索性配对评价。

**实现验证。** 6 项 `tests/test_phase_c3a_flow.py` 定向测试通过；3 个 Python 文件通过 `py_compile`，协议 JSON 解析和 `git diff --check` 通过。另在真实冻结 train/development 中各取 1 个身份做 CPU 通路检查：train 完成一次反向传播，development 完成 8 步 Heun 推理；无指标进入选择。检查发现固定退化的 `upper_clamp=false`，SAR LR 可超过 1（一个 train 样本抽查值域 0.0226–2.5269）；实现保留该范围，不裁剪 LR，目标仍按固定输入约束在 `[0,1]`。

**资源阻塞。** 首次 GPU 启动预检报告 RTX 3050 空闲显存 1.06/3.67 GiB，后续五次提权预检均检测到 3 个其他 CUDA 计算进程。最近一次使用仓库指定的 `AISAR` 环境运行预登记入口，训练器以退出码 2 在读图前停止；没有创建训练目录、读取影像或开始 CUDA 训练。证据见[预检 v1](../../experiments/diagnostics/c3a_gpu_preflight_20261105_v1/manifest.json)、[预检 v2](../../experiments/diagnostics/c3a_gpu_preflight_20261105_v2/manifest.json)、[预检 v3](../../experiments/diagnostics/c3a_gpu_preflight_20261105_v3/manifest.json)、[预检 v4](../../experiments/diagnostics/c3a_gpu_preflight_20261105_v4/manifest.json)、[预检 v5](../../experiments/diagnostics/c3a_gpu_preflight_20261105_v5/manifest.json)及[预检 v6](../../experiments/diagnostics/c3a_gpu_preflight_20261105_v6/manifest.json)。本轮 C3-A `confirmation_payload_accessed=false`、`test_set_accessed=false`、`test_set_used=false`、`optical_payload_read=false`。C2 v2 原 gate 仍“未判定”，B1 仍 `not_promoted / stop_at_b1`；本探索不构成 C3 阶段晋级或生成质量证据。

## C1 v2 confirmation 上的 C2 一次性复核 — 2026-11-05（访问后修订；非晋级证据）

暂存清单位于 [`数据暂存 manifest`](../../data/derived/c1_v2_confirmation_20261105_v1/manifest.json)，含 200 个样本、20 个来源组及 400 个影像文件；逐文件 SHA-512、PNG 模式和尺寸核验通过。四条件固定输入位于 `data/derived/c2_confirmation_inputs_20261105_v1/`，生成清单见[`固定输入执行 manifest`](../../data/derived/c2_confirmation_inputs_20261105_v1/execution_manifest.json)。

原协议 [`protocol.json`](../../protocols/mmcanet_phase_c2/c2_confirmation_20261105_v1/protocol.json) 的 SHA-256 为 `b46df6eb33f5b4e122868d337b82441db5263c340610bf5a3e220e09f572c523`。两次访问后修订分别为 [`修订一`](../../protocols/mmcanet_phase_c2/c2_confirmation_20261105_v1/protocol_amendment_20261105_v1.json)（`31d27e3f2b2f70d57b9fb726c1f48e34f9faa0348e48e95eba188d1b823fe77d`）和[`修订二`](../../protocols/mmcanet_phase_c2/c2_confirmation_20261105_v1/protocol_amendment_20261105_v2.json)（`ef9fe360c0cc19aa5f72eb2ad8af07381c1bb333f5e71267349498ae8437267c`）。两次修订只修复已冻结路径和阈值字段的读取；无指标值参与修正，样本、模型、checkpoint、指标、阈值和判定均未改变。

第一次失败发生在推理前，记录见[首轮执行清单](../../experiments/phase_c2_confirmation_c1_v2_20261105_v1/execution_manifest.json)；第二次完成四条件推理但在结果写出前失败，清单见[`amended_v1`执行记录](../../experiments/phase_c2_confirmation_c1_v2_20261105_amended_v1/execution_manifest.json)。最终结果位于[`amended_v2`恢复运行](../../experiments/phase_c2_confirmation_c1_v2_20261105_amended_v2/)，包含 3,200 行逐图控制指标、1,600 行配对对比和 72 行组汇总；逐图结果哈希均由 `execution_manifest.json` 绑定。报告中的来源组等权 95% bootstrap 区间不把 patch 当作独立样本。由于执行修订发生在 confirmation 载荷访问之后，最终状态 `inconclusive` 只作描述性复核，不能作为原定正式晋级确认。

## C2 v2 正式评价失败与非门槛补充诊断 — 2026-10-31

原评价运行位于[screen 目录](../../experiments/phase_c2_c1_v2_20261031_v1/screen/)，执行 manifest 记录 `ValueError`：`Target background median is zero or too small for a defined ratio`。输入诊断发现只有 2 个唯一 development 身份出现零背景中位数；因条件掩膜不同形成 6 条条件×样本记录（`x2_light` 2 条、`x2_severe` 2 条、`x4_light` 1 条、`x4_severe` 1 条）。这些图的背景均值仍为正，但背景像素中 53.1%–71.2% 为精确零。C2 v2 规定任一必需指标未定义时整次评价失败，所以该 gate 结果必须保持“未判定”；没有加 epsilon、删样本或更改冻结协议。

为保留方法观察，另建[非门槛补充诊断](../../experiments/phase_c2_c1_v2_20261031_v1/non_gate_diagnostic_20261031_v1/diagnostic_report.md)：保留全部 200 个 development 身份、19 个来源组和四种推理控制，生成 3,200 条逐图控制、3,200 条配对对比；只将两项 median TBR 误差标为不可用，不生成 gate decision。四条件 matched optical 相对 SAR-only 的来源组 PSNR 增益为 −0.0055、+0.0042、+0.0061、−0.0024 dB，各 95% 来源组 bootstrap 区间均跨零，且均低于预设 +0.05 dB 继续阈值；matched 相对 permuted optical 的 PSNR 增益为 +0.0099 至 +0.0152 dB，四个区间下界均高于零。该结果仅是单 seed、开发集描述性观察；不构成冻结 gate、稳定效用或独立泛化结论。

## C2 配对评价执行链与 GPU 资源复核 — 2026-10-31（取得载荷前的中间快照，已由后续实验更新）

新增 [C2 screen 执行入口](../../scripts/evaluate_phase_c2_screen.py) 与配对统计/结果 writer。执行前核验八组训练矩阵、best checkpoint、初始化哈希、四条件开发输入 manifest 与来源组置换；推理只接受 development 数据。通过合成样本验证固定掩膜哈希、四控制对比、等权来源组 bootstrap、决策规则、中文报告和访问标记。

提权 AISAR 下 9 个 C2 定向测试文件共 59 项通过。冻结 C2 模型在合成 `[1,1,128,128]` SAR 和 `[1,3,256,256]` optical 输入上的 CUDA 推理预检通过，项目数据未读取。此前一次 batch=8 合成训练预检因仅剩约 22.94 MiB 而 OOM；2026-10-31 重试时，运行前可用显存为 470.9 MiB，冻结合成训练步通过，峰值分配 365.8 MiB、运行后余 52.9 MiB。该预检只证明一批合成数据的一步优化可执行。候选 `/mnt/data`、`/mnt/winC`、`/mnt/winD`、`/mnt/winE` 均为空；共享数据目录仅见 MNIST。C1 冻结 train/development 源文件仍未找到，未开始真实输入生成、训练或模型评价。

详见 [C2 代码与资源审计](../../experiments/diagnostics/c2_execution_acceptance_20261031_v1/report.md)、[本轮文本暴露审计](../../experiments/data_audits/research_text_read_exposure_20261031_v1/report.md)、D-2026-10-31-001/002/003 和 [执行验收计划](../superpowers/plans/2026-10-30-c2-runner-acceptance.md)。

## 自适应参考引导相关工作 — 2026-10-30

一手来源审查将 Ada-RefSR 记录为自然图像扩散式参考超分辨率的直接先例：它使用自适应参考门控，并报告无关参考、模糊和仿射错配压力测试。STMP-DiT 是按阶段组织多模态先验的相关工作，作者报告过语义描述错误导致企鹅翅膀生成岩石纹理的案例。两者都未评估 SAR 输出恢复、JDSR、雷达保真度或 SAR 回退。通用学习门控或自适应参考抑制不能单独构成新颖性主张；C5 仍是任务特定假设。矩阵现有 142 行：78 行已确认属于旧清单，另有 64 行的旧清单归属未确认。候选 v1.2 仍待批准；B1 仍为 not_promoted / stop_at_b1；C2 执行验收未完成；B2/B3 仍受门控。没有运行项目实验或模型。宽范围仓库文本搜索可能遍历历史测试相关文本；当前及累计测试标记仍为 test_set_accessed=true、test_set_used=false，未使用任何结果。见 D-2026-10-30-004 和[文献审查](../../experiments/literature_audits/adaptive_reference_guidance_prior_art_20261030_v1/report.md)。

## 条件流逆问题相关工作审查 — 2026-10-30

ICML 2026 的 Variational Flow Maps（VFM）是条件图像逆问题的相关先例，论文在已知算子和加性高斯观测噪声下报告了 ImageNet ×4 超分辨率结果。这缩小了通用条件流映射恢复的可主张新颖性，但不能验证 SAR 观测似然或光学引导的 SAR 输出恢复。本地数据审计仅能确认存在带噪归一化伪目标；冻结的 C2 定义了表示域中的 FFT、降采样和 Gamma 合成链。基于该链已解析推导候选连续域密度，包括零值支撑行为及大于 1 的合法取值；完整 VFM 变分目标和有限精度处理仍未验证。该模型描述的是对伪目标的表示域恢复，不是干净 SAR 恢复或物理一致性。NeurIPS 2025 的 Blockwise Flow Matching（BFM）仅提供通用生成效率先例。该次记录时矩阵共 140 行，其中 78 行确认属于旧清单，62 行归属未确认。候选 v1.2 仍待批准；B1 为 not_promoted / stop_at_b1；C2 执行验收未完成；B2/B3 仍受门控。未访问 SAR 图像、测试/确认集数据、实验指标、checkpoint 或预测；未运行或修改项目代码、模型。累计测试标记为 test_set_accessed=true、test_set_used=false。见 D-2026-10-30-001/002/003 和[来源审查](../../experiments/literature_audits/flow_inverse_generation_prior_art_20261030_v1/report.md)。

本节是当前状态入口。以下按日期保留研究与审计历史；其中矩阵计数和结论仅适用于各自记录的日期。

## C2 metric contract has runner-level acceptance gaps — 2026-10-29

Static comparison of frozen C2 v2 and its metric helpers found that the ratio helper's effective `eps=1e-8` is inherited from source rather than serialized in the protocol; the helper can return a null reported edge-correlation metric that the C2 wrapper passes through; and the mask builder returns thresholds/masks without area coverage. ## Current SEN1-2 cohort does not support a physical SAR consistency claim — 2026-10-29

The retained TUM SEN1-2 supporting document describes SAR patches as 8-bit single-channel sigma-nought dB, with no byte-to-dB transfer table. C1 v2's selected train/development PNGs were byte-matched to official source and decode as `uint8`; this establishes lineage and storage type, not calibrated values. The separate earlier 1,800-pair B1 v2 profile likewise has no raw calibrated values, and its synthetic Gamma multiplier acts on a normalized noisy pseudo-target in representation space. The documented PNG source and B1 v2 protocol therefore support only representation-domain claims, not calibrated physical likelihood, sensor-look, absolute-radiometry, or physical-speckle claims. Keep the charter's “physics-consistent” title conditional on authoritative measurement-domain data and a validated forward/noise model; use representation-domain language for work on these PNGs. Candidate v1.2 remains pending approval; B1 remains `not_promoted / stop_at_b1`, C2 execution acceptance is incomplete, and B2/B3 remain gated. No gate, protocol, model, or historical result changed. This review opened no image/test/confirmation payload. See D-2026-10-29-024.

## Flow posterior-transport literature review — 2026-10-29

Full primary-source review covered FlowDPS (ICCV 2025 author manuscript) and the 2026 posterior-transport arXiv preprint. The latter treats exact conditioning under a deterministic diffeomorphic flow as source reweighting, bounds approximate correction bias under assumptions, and reports a controlled 2D FlowDPS-style guidance case with posterior-mode errors. Its high-dimensional source importance sampler is intractable, and its image-scale solver is described as heuristic rather than posterior-certified. The reviewed works use general image inverse problems, not SAR multiplicative-noise restoration or optical-conditioned SAR. The matrix now has 132 rows/12 columns: 78 historical-membership confirmed and 54 unresolved. No hypothesis, charter status, gate, data protocol, model, or experiment changed. The candidate remains inactive pending approval; C2 execution acceptance is incomplete; B1 remains `not_promoted / stop_at_b1`; B2/B3 remain gated. Current-task test flags are false/false; cumulative project flags remain true/false. See D-2026-10-29-013 and the [audit](../../experiments/literature_audits/flow_posterior_transport_source_review_20261029_v1/report.md).

The related-work matrix now has 118 rows: 78 with direct pre-corruption membership evidence and 40 additions with unresolved historical membership. Ma et al.'s flood-mapping preprint row was restored using the explicit contemporaneous D-2026-10-18-09 inclusion record; its ±2-pixel tolerance is for SAR flood predictions against S2-derived labels, while its CVAE is SAR-only despeckling. Neither evaluates optical-guided SAR image restoration. Against the documented 125-row lower bound, at least 7 original rows remain unaccounted for; if the original inventory had exactly 125 rows, 7–47 original rows are absent. The new Lian et al. 2026 *Remote Sensing* paper is publisher-indexed registration prior art for structure-confidence-weighted phase congruency and coarse-to-fine matching. The official page's indexed text was usable, but direct HTML/PDF/XML routes were unavailable, so details remain abstract/selected-indexed-text level. A follow-up author-copy search was clipped and excluded; the OpenAlex metadata route was inaccessible and an exact-title arXiv search returned no results. Keep full-text details unresolved. See D-2026-10-28-030/032 and the [audit](../../experiments/literature_audits/registration_structure_confidence_phase_congruency_20261028_v1/report.md), plus D-2026-10-28-061 and the [row-restoration audit](../../experiments/literature_audits/flood_mapping_shift_invariant_row_restore_20261028_v1/report.md).

The three direct optical-guided SAR-despeckling rows now have explicit code/data link status: the 2019 GNLM entry links its author repository (previously recorded in D-2026-10-18-07); the reviewed 2015 author article/institutional record yielded no verified code route; the Gaya 2024 official program lists no resources and its reviewed institutional abstract has no code link. Gaya remains abstract-level. No experimental or B1/B2/B3 status changed; see the [GNLM repository status audit](../../experiments/literature_audits/gnlm_author_repository_status_recheck_20261028_v1/report.md) and D-2026-10-26-046.

The bibliography-to-matrix crosswalk restored Shibata et al. (2017) and Bungert & Ehrhardt (2020), whose earlier inclusion was documented in D-2026-10-25-107 but whose rows were absent from the current CSV, and added Hughes & Schmitt (2019) as abstract-level SAR–optical matching prior art. The first two have existing primary-source review; the Hughes paper's official abstract is available but its PDF timed out. These works narrow generic misalignment novelty claims without establishing SAR restoration utility or changing H1/B1. See D-2026-10-28-043 and the [crosswalk audit](../../experiments/literature_audits/bibliography_matrix_crosswalk_20261028_v1/report.md).

Amao-Oliva et al.'s 2025 ADAM paper is newly indexed as adjacent SAR joint-processing prior art: it jointly optimizes despeckling and compression and compares against MERLIN→JPEG2000. The authors' quantitative Hamburg reference averages 21 co-registered HH intensity acquisitions and may contain temporal-change error; the reviewed text does not establish source-group-disjoint train/test allocation. ADAM does not perform SR or use optical guidance, so it is not a task-matched local baseline and does not change the B1 `not_promoted` / `stop_at_b1` decision. See D-2026-10-28-036 and the [full-text audit](../../experiments/literature_audits/adam_joint_compression_despeckling_20261028_v1/report.md).

De Paepe et al.'s Sim2Real paper is adjacent SAR-only joint despeckling and sidelobe-reduction prior art. It trains on MOCEM simulations; the authors describe reflectivity as an effective, not physically measurable, target and say the background maps provide context rather than true radar backscatter. Real Umbra/Capella results include ENL and qualitative sidelobe assessment, with no quantitative sidelobe metric. The paper does not address SR or optical guidance, and its historical membership in the pre-corruption matrix is unresolved. It does not change the B1 `not_promoted` / `stop_at_b1` gate. See D-2026-10-28-040 and the [full-text audit](../../experiments/literature_audits/sim2real_joint_despeckling_sidelobes_20261028_v1/report.md).

The bounded literature-review crosswalk added four current-evidence rows: Capella SAR SR (synthetic bicubic degradation), 3MOS and SOMA-1M alignment datasets, and SAR400 as a conditional despeckling-data candidate. Their limits do not establish source-group independence, optical-guided SAR restoration utility, or a task-matched local baseline. See the [crosswalk audit](../../experiments/literature_audits/related_work_matrix_literature_review_crosswalk_20261028_v1/report.md) and D-2026-10-28-041.

DPS1-SAR is now recorded as a conditional SAR-only despeckling dataset candidate. Its official record lists six GeoTIFF ZIP packages for noisy VV/VH inputs and temporal mean/median references, but file access requires an IEEE DataPort subscription. The available method description does not establish input-excluded references or source-group-disjoint splits. This does not unblock local provenance or model selection and does not change H1 or the B1 gate. See D-2026-10-28-042 and the [source screen](../../experiments/literature_audits/dps1_sar_source_screen_20261028_v1/report.md).

The Wiley full text says the entire test set is used for OMA-SSR's training loss (Sec. 2.4) while separately defining train/test splits (Sec. 3.1). The article does not verify code behavior, and wording/translation error remains possible. Do not use those reported test metrics as independent evidence until a primary correction or implementation clarifies the data flow. This is a literature finding only; local gates are unchanged. ## CPU/CUDA reproducibility check (2026-10-27)

The v2 CPU and v3 CUDA per-sample outputs were paired across 1,200 rows by seed, validation identity, source folder, and optical condition. Both runs have the same fixed-input manifest hash, input-binding hash, and all three checkpoint hashes. Maximum absolute differences were `4.57e-9` for prediction-delta RMS, `6.49e-9` for relative prediction-delta RMS, `2.64e-6` for fusion-feature delta RMS, and `6.09e-6` for direct optical-context input RMS; the SAR-base delta matched exactly in all rows. No tolerance or promotion threshold was applied. This is an implementation reproducibility check for one interpretation-only diagnostic, not an independent replicate, quality result, or gate evidence. See D-2026-10-27-047 and the [comparison audit](../../experiments/diagnostics/b1_v2_cpu_cuda_output_reproducibility_20261027_v1/report.md).

The AIP Advances dual-channel SAR–optical registration paper is a current-evidence row under D-2026-10-27-008. It is registration prior art only; its pair-level split and unspecified parent groups/reference identities do not establish local registration accuracy or a restoration mismatch threshold. SAG-CR and CGD-Net are now also indexed as current-evidence, reverse-task rows: both reconstruct optical outputs and do not establish SAR-output fidelity or SAR-only fallback. The matrix now has 103 rows: 77 with direct evidence of pre-corruption membership and 26 additions whose original membership is unresolved. ## Interpretation-only direct fusion-input bypass (2026-10-27)

The [v2 diagnostic](../../experiments/diagnostics/b1_v2_optical_direct_fusion_bypass_20261027_v2/runs/attempt_001/report.md) used the same 200 development-validation identities, three C2 EMA seeds, 15 recorded source folders, and matched/frozen-permuted optical conditions. It set only the direct optical-HR and optical-context channel groups entering `fusion_stem` to zero; both cross-attention updates remained active. The 1,200 per-identity rows and 30 source-folder summaries were finite, all nine output hashes matched, and test flags were false/false. Folder-equal prediction delta relative RMS was 0.0002428 (0.02428%) matched and 0.0002428 (0.02428%) permuted; SAR-base delta was exactly zero. The direct fusion features changed substantially more than the final prediction, consistent with the downstream reliability/residual/high-pass path limiting output change on these fixed checkpoints. The intervention is out of training distribution and has no targets or quality metrics; it is not optical-utility, quality, or robustness evidence and cannot select/promote a model.

The separate v1 attempt failed before model inference because its fixed-input path did not equal the absolute path in `input_binding.json`; it is retained as a preflight failure only. Its temporary inputs, duplicate attempt copies, bytecode, and raw logs were removed. The whole-file `verification.json` digest mismatch remains unresolved even though v2 validated the expected payload hashes. B1 stays `not_promoted`; B2/B3 remain gated.

## Verified B1 v2 evidence

The [aggregate report](../../experiments/sen12_scene_dev1800_b1_revalidation_20261023_source_aligned_v1/aggregate/report.md) and [aggregate manifest](../../experiments/sen12_scene_dev1800_b1_revalidation_20261023_source_aligned_v1/aggregate/aggregate_manifest.json) record six runs across seeds 0–2, 15 source groups in the fall-only `ROIs1970_fall` ROI-season, and run-level `test_set_accessed=false`, `test_set_used=false`. Mean paired ΔPSNR is +0.001617 dB (source-group bootstrap 95% CI [+0.000075, +0.003180]); mean ΔSSIM is −0.000892 (95% CI [−0.001113, −0.000675]). The protocol records `not_promoted`. Registration is nominal/source-matched; fine pixel co-registration is unverified. This is development evidence within one ROI-season, not geographic or acquisition generalization evidence.

A read-only secondary-metric group analysis found EPI correlation increased in all 15 source groups, while edge-strength and bright-peak ratio errors increased in all 15. Mean TBR ratio error increased in all 15 group means; bright integrated-energy ratio error and bright-region MAE increased in 12/15 and improved in 3/15. This is a metric trade-off observation, not a causal explanation; see the [diagnostic report](../../experiments/diagnostics/b1_v2_multimetric_group_tradeoffs_20261026_v1/report.md). The frozen gate remains unchanged.

## Interpretation-only optical-pairing diagnostic (2026-10-26)

A zero-training comparison covered matched, cross-source-group permuted, and missing optical inputs plus the SAR-only checkpoint, using 200 fixed validation identities, 15 fall-only source groups, and seeds 0–2. Matched versus permuted optical had near-zero PSNR/SSIM differences with both source-group intervals crossing zero. Matched versus missing optical showed a small PSNR increase (+0.001621 dB; 95% CI [+0.000079, +0.003153]) alongside lower SSIM (−0.000893; 95% CI [−0.001112, −0.000681]) and worse edge/TBR/bright-peak ratio errors. Matched versus SAR-only showed the same trade-off. The result does not show stable metric-level benefit from correct optical pairing and does not prove the predictions are identical; direct prediction differences are now quantified in the follow-up below. See the [diagnostic report](../../experiments/diagnostics/b1_v2_optical_pairing_sensitivity_20261026_v1/report.md) and D-2026-10-26-130.

Run-bound `config.json`, `files.csv`, `manifest.json`, and `split.txt` hashes match the retained metadata for both train and validation. The raw `verification.json` SHA-256 recorded in `input_binding.json` does not match either retained verifier record. The adjacent `input_verification.json` says `frozen_metadata_byte_identical=true`, but does not define whether that covers the verifier-output files. The validation diagnostics independently checked all 1,400 rebuilt per-sample payload hashes against run-bound `files.csv`, and they matched. The run commands say the full per-file verifier outputs were temporary and removed after their hashes were recorded; this likely explains the digest mismatch, but the original bytes are unavailable to confirm it. See the [metadata-only recheck](../../experiments/data_audits/b1_v2_verification_record_hash_recheck_20261026_v1/report.md).

## Prediction-difference norm diagnostic (2026-10-26)

The zero-training follow-up compared full, unmasked outputs for 200 fixed validation identities, 15 source groups, and seeds 0–2. Mean relative RMS was 0.0105% for matched versus permuted optical, 0.1291% for matched versus missing optical, and 0.1290% for matched optical versus the SAR-only checkpoint. Outputs were not identical under permutation, but pairing changed them only slightly on this cohort. These values are in the normalized representation output domain, not physical SAR units; the source-group intervals condition on the three fixed seeds. The matched-versus-SAR-only contrast also changes checkpoint and architecture. This does not prove optical information has no value or establish safe fallback. B1 remains not_promoted; B2/B3 remain gated. See the [norm report](../../experiments/diagnostics/b1_v2_prediction_difference_norms_20261026_v1/report.md), which also records the unresolved verification.json digest discrepancy.

A static EMA-checkpoint audit of the three historical C2 seeds found matching checkpoint SHA-256 values in their manifests. All use the `MMCANetV2` high-frequency residual head with cross-attention, no learned alignment, and fixed residual/output scales of 1.0. The final residual projection was initialized at zero; its EMA weight RMS is 0.00450–0.00511 across seeds, while the final reliability-head weight RMS is 0.1378–0.1671. This is consistent with a small learned residual update, but parameter magnitudes alone do not quantify optical contribution. That parameter-only audit did not compute gate activations; a later interpretation-only activation audit is recorded below. See the [parameter audit](../../experiments/diagnostics/b1_v2_optical_candidate_head_parameter_audit_20261026_v1/report.md).

## Optical gate activation diagnostic (2026-10-26; interpretation-only)

A predeclared zero-training audit used `return_aux=True` on the three existing C2 EMA checkpoints, comparing matched optical with the same cross-source-group permutation as the prior pairing diagnostic. It covered 200 fixed validation identities, 15 source groups in `ROIs1970_fall`, and seeds 0–2. The group-balanced reliability mean was 0.4667 matched and 0.4667 permuted; the paired reliability-map RMS difference was 0.00892. The gated residual contribution/base RMS ratio was 0.001291 (0.129%) matched and 0.001290 (0.129%) permuted; the paired contribution-map RMS difference was 8.15e-5 in normalized representation units. The SAR base was identical across optical conditions.

The gate output was not near zero on this cohort, while the effective residual correction was small. This is consistent with a low-amplitude residual path, but does not isolate its cause, establish optical utility, or prove optical information has no value. These are group-balanced descriptive summaries without a promotion threshold or inferential interval. The validation cohort remains contaminated development data from one ROI-season, and fine registration remains unverified. Target/mask values and quality metrics were not used; 1,400 validation payload hashes were checked. The separate whole-file `verification.json` digest mismatch remains unresolved. Task flags are false/false; B1 remains `not_promoted`; B2/B3 remain gated. See the [protocol](../../experiments/diagnostics/b1_v2_optical_gate_activation_20261026_v1/protocol.json) and [report](../../experiments/diagnostics/b1_v2_optical_gate_activation_20261026_v1/report.md).

## Data and evaluation boundaries

The project `data/sar` and `data/opt` trees contain a 2,000-pair project-labelled PNG subset; source/release lineage is not verified, so do not call it the complete official SEN1-2 release. Earlier fixed train/validation and B1F fit/inner-dev overlap audits rule out those partitions as independent model-selection evidence. The B1 v2 ROI grouping is still limited to one fall ROI-season. A 2026-10-26 recheck of the official QXS-SAROPT author repository and issues exposed no file-level city/source map or alternate public mirror; it does not identify the local subset as QXS. Test remains prohibited for selection. Per-run B1 v2 flags are false/false; cumulative project disclosure flags remain `test_set_accessed=true`, `test_set_used=false`.

## PNG container metadata lineage audit (2026-10-26)

A metadata-only scan covered 2,000 PNGs each in `data/sar` and `data/opt`. All 4,000 files shared 256×256 dimensions and 8-bit depth; the two directories had 2,000 exact basename matches (`8000`–`9999`). The containers had only `IHDR`, `IDAT`, and `IEND` chunks, with no text or ancillary metadata; neither root contains sidecar files or nested directories. This confirms the local PNGs do not embed source, acquisition, product-level, or registration lineage, but it does not identify their release or rule out manifests elsewhere. The scan skipped compressed pixel payloads and did not touch test paths. See the [audit report](../../experiments/data_audits/png_header_lineage_20261026_v1/report.md).

## Published dataset lineage crosswalk (2026-10-26)

Primary papers report different canonical tilings: SEN1-2 uses 256×256 patches at stride 128 (50% overlap), while QXS-SAROPT reports about 20% overlap but describes it as a “stride of 52,” which is ambiguous under the usual step-size convention. The local 204–205-pixel offsets and 51–52-pixel shared strips therefore do not match canonical SEN1-2 tiling if preserved, but are compatible with a 20%-overlap extraction. This is not provenance: local retiling could explain either, and the local source/acquisition map is absent. A SEN12MS follow-on paper says the SEN1-2 distribution it describes lacked geolocation metadata. See the [source crosswalk](../../experiments/data_audits/dataset_lineage_source_paper_crosswalk_20261026_v1/report.md).

The SEN1-2 author manuscript also reports GRD IW VV σ⁰ dB, orbit/DEM orthorectification, no speckle filtering, Sentinel-2 RGB bands 4/3/2, and per-band contrast stretching. It describes 258 scenes/ROIs, 256×256 patches at stride 128, 298,790 pairs before manual removal of 16,406, and 282,384 final pairs. These published collection facts do not map the local 2k PNGs or establish their radiometry, preprocessing, or fine registration. See D-2026-10-26-136 and the [full-text crosswalk](../../experiments/data_audits/dataset_lineage_source_paper_crosswalk_20261026_v1/report.md).

The author manuscript also calls the collection co-registered, describes scene/season deterministic splits as a way to create independent data, and says its Sentinel-2 source was L1C/TOA at the time. It reports no independent S1-to-S2 checkpoint residuals for the local cohort. These statements do not authenticate local file identity, temporal pairing, fine alignment, or independent split membership; see D-2026-10-26-137.

**SEN12MS geospatial utility route (2026-10-26):** The pinned author notebook derives a coordinate per parsed region from the CRS and affine transform of available SEN12MS label GeoTIFFs, then writes a shapefile at runtime. It provides no static mapping from SEN12MS region IDs to SEN1-2 source folders or to the local numeric 2k filenames. An open community issue reports that region locations differ from the notebook example and some remain misplaced after an x/y swap; the generated map is not independent geospatial validation. This route does not resolve local source groups or fine registration; see the [source audit](../../experiments/literature_audits/sen12ms_geospatial_utility_route_20261026_v1/report.md).

## CDSE Sentinel-1 IW SLC metadata route (2026-10-26)

The frozen, asset-free screen returned 12/12 HTTP 200 responses. It found 50 candidate datatake pairs across 10 active AOIs, yielding 10 disconnected components under the predeclared shared-datatake/AOI-overlap rule; two AOIs had no pair. This meets the eight-component floor for a separate metadata-only rights, calibration, cloud/time, geolocation/registration, and target-protocol review. It does not establish source-ground-truth independence, pairing validity, or a usable SLC learning target. No assets, pixels, local data, split, test payload, or test metric were accessed. B1 remains `not_promoted` / `stop_at_b1`; B2/B3 remain gated. See [D-2026-10-26-146](DECISION_LOG.md#d-2026-10-26-146--advance-the-slc-iw-route-to-asset-free-protocol-review) and the [audit report](../../experiments/data_audits/cdse_slc_iw_aoi_group_feasibility_20261026_v1/report.md).

## Next actions

1. Continue source/product provenance and precise local registration evidence; the local PNGs still lack parent-scene/acquisition mapping and independent fine-alignment measurements.
2. Keep optical-use causality open. The attention and direct fusion-input interventions are out of training distribution and not additive; do not repeat them or use the overlapping validation cohort for selection. B1 stays `not_promoted`; do not enter B2/B3.
3. For future model-forward diagnostics and training, require a GPU preflight and use GPU when available. Do not silently fall back to CPU; if the driver remains unavailable, defer compute and continue provenance/literature work.
4. Recover the original title/DOI inventory only from direct evidence. The 119-row read predates six additions, making 125 the known pre-corruption lower bound; the exact final count remains unknown. Do not repeat exhausted local filename, Git-object, or Drive-search routes without changed state.
5. Do not inspect test images or read test metrics.

The next prerequisite is authorized metadata mapping unused source-scene folders to geographic/acquisition identifiers across additional roots. See [D-2026-10-26-015](DECISION_LOG.md#d-2026-10-26-015--record-conditional-source-folder-bootstrap-uncertainty) and the [diagnostic](../../experiments/diagnostics/sen12_scene_dev1800_b1_group_bootstrap_20261026_v1/report.md).

**SEN1-2 source-folder semantics (2026-10-26):** The official paper defines each top-level `ROIs<seed>_<season>` directory as one random ROI-sampling batch paired with a season; its second-level `s1_i`/`s2_i` directories are source-scene folders. Thus the current validation uses 15 source scenes in the `ROIs1970_fall` batch-season root, not one physical ROI. Source-folder disjointness is supported, but exact geographic separation and acquisition independence remain unverified. See [D-2026-10-26-016](DECISION_LOG.md#d-2026-10-26-016--clarify-sen1-2-root-and-scene-folder-units) and the [audit addendum](../../experiments/data_audits/sen12_scene_dev1800_parent_roi_feasibility_20261025_v1/report.md#addendum--official-folder-hierarchy-clarification-2026-10-26).

**Noisier2Noise known-noise boundary (2026-10-25):** The method trains from one noisy realization only when the source corruption model is known and sampleable; its non-additive derivation is for multiplicative Bernoulli masking, not general Gamma speckle. The local normalized PNG source-noise law remains unestablished, and the LR/HR pseudo-target share one source observation. No clean-SAR route, run, or gate change follows. See [D-2026-10-25-179](DECISION_LOG.md#d-2026-10-25-179--bound-noisier2noise-to-known-source-noise-models) and the [audit](../../experiments/literature_audits/noisier2noise_known_noise_boundary_20261025_v1/report.md).

**Noise2Noise population conditional-mean clarification (2026-10-25):** Lehtinen et al.'s Eq. 6 states an indexed clean-target condition. In population form, unrestricted squared-loss estimators match when E[Z|X]=E[S|X]; target noise with zero conditional mean given S and independent of X given S is sufficient, and this conditional mean need not equal a sample-specific S. The local fixed-input builder derives LR and HR pseudo-target from the same preprocessed SAR observation, so no clean-target residual relation is established; local metrics remain noisy-pseudo-target consistency, not clean-SAR evidence. Source-only clarification; no local payload, run, experiment, or gate changed. See [D-2026-10-25-178](DECISION_LOG.md#d-2026-10-25-178--clarify-noise2noise-conditional-means-in-the-population-formulation) and the [audit](../../experiments/literature_audits/noise2noise_population_mean_clarification_20261025_v1/report.md).

Discussion analyses cloud-cover levels and land-cover categories. No explicit geometric-shift or measured residual-registration evaluation was located in the accessible article text, and the output is cloud-free optical imagery. This is adjacent fusion prior art, not SAR-output mismatch-robustness or fallback evidence. Publisher-reported performance text surfaced but was not used. Added this boundary to the claim evidence map; the broader code-to-experiment map remains incomplete. No local data or experiment changed. See [D-2026-10-25-176](DECISION_LOG.md#d-2026-10-25-176--classify-mamba-scr-as-optical-output-fusion-without-explicit-geometric-stress-evidence) and the [audit](../../experiments/literature_audits/mamba_scr_sen12ms_cr_split_route_20261025_v1/report.md).

**Optional anisotropic-blur characterization (2026-10-25):** Two synthetic tests now confirm that a centered impulse response is normalized, a 90° parameter rotation swaps its image-axis spread, and zero padding attenuates a constant image at the corner. The focused degradation suite passes 13/13. This characterizes current code; it does not validate zero padding as a physical SAR boundary rule or identify x/y with range/azimuth. No local data, project test cohort, run, or checkpoint was accessed. Cumulative access flags remain `test_set_accessed=true`, `test_set_used=false`. See [D-2026-10-25-169](DECISION_LOG.md#d-2026-10-25-169--characterize-optional-blur-with-synthetic-tests) and the [audit](../../experiments/data_audits/anisotropic_blur_characterization_20261025_v1/report.md).

**Canonical degradation audit (2026-10-25):** The audited HR→LR helper applies centered FFT low-pass, optional anisotropic Gaussian blur, bilinear resizing, an explicit nonnegative policy, optional Gamma multiplication, and optional output clipping. Its default speckle domain is `representation`; the sampler's intensity multiplier is `Gamma(L, rate=L)`, while the amplitude option uses its square root. Eleven focused synthetic tests passed across canonical imports, normalization, FFT properties, and Gamma moments. This does not establish physical ENL or local SAR noise fidelity: the inputs are normalized PNG-derived noisy pseudo-targets, and the optional blur uses zero padding with image-axis orientation that still needs a protocol-specific interpretation. A bounded README read surfaced split-role metadata and historical summary text; no value was used, and no image/metric/checkpoint payload was opened. Cumulative flags remain `test_set_accessed=true`, `test_set_used=false`. See [D-2026-10-25-168](DECISION_LOG.md#d-2026-10-25-168--bound-the-canonical-degradation-to-representation-domain-evidence) and the [audit](../../experiments/data_audits/canonical_degradation_semantics_20261025_v1/report.md).

**SAR-DDPM primary-text and code audit (2026-10-25):** The author manuscript establishes conditional diffusion for SAR despeckling, trained with synthetic Gamma-speckled optical imagery and evaluated synthetically plus by ENL on two real Sentinel-1 scenes; it does not test inference-time optical guidance, SR, or joint restoration. The author-linked mutable main repository is not bound to the reported runs and has static conditioning/configuration inconsistencies. Keep it as narrow despeckling prior art and keep local diffusion deferred. No local data, result, test asset, model, or gate changed. **So2Sat LCZ42 training-metadata screen (2026-10-25):** The author-associated v4 training geolocation file contains 352,366 patches across 42 city labels and 26 EPSG codes. The within-EPSG footprint check found no positive-area cross-city overlap. A common-CRS envelope screen found one candidate among 835 different-EPSG city pairs; a patch-level attempt was invalidated because 9,986 transformed patch boundaries were non-convex, so no exact overlap count is accepted. City labels therefore do not certify a globally non-overlapping split or independent acquisitions. The dataset is a 32×32 classification benchmark without a clean restoration target, patch-level product/date lineage, or measured residual registration; it remains unadopted. The pinned commit page incidentally exposed only the testing geolocation file's LFS hash and size; no test identity, coordinate, image, label, or metric was accessed or used. Cumulative flags remain `test_set_accessed=true`, `test_set_used=false`. B1 remains `not_promoted`; B2/B3 remain gated. **Noisy pseudo-target Bayes-risk clarification (2026-10-25):** For any square-integrable decomposition `T=X+E`, the population squared-error gains from adding optical input satisfy `ΔR_T=ΔR_X+E||δ_E||²+2E⟨δ_X,δ_E⟩`, where each δ is the change in the corresponding conditional mean from `(Y)` to `(Y,O)`. Their ordering is unrestricted unless the residual-mean terms are controlled. The local shared-input pipeline does not establish that condition; this identity adds no clean-SAR empirical evidence or finite-network correction. A bounded research-context read was truncated and excluded from evidence; no historical metric value was used for this derivation. Cumulative flags remain `test_set_accessed=true`, `test_set_used=false`. B1 remains `not_promoted`; B2/B3 remain gated, and no run or experiment changed. See [D-2026-10-25-152](DECISION_LOG.md#d-2026-10-25-152--record-the-exact-noisy-pseudo-target-bayes-risk-decomposition) and the [derivation](optical_information_analysis.md#exact-bayes-risk-decomposition-for-a-noisy-pseudo-target-2026-10-25).

Folder, commit-history, and API routes were inaccessible, so no manuscript or pinned source was reviewed. Keep OGSFlow at mutable README-level prior-art status; this adds no paper-level or performance claim. No local data, code, model, or test asset was accessed. B1 remains `not_promoted`; B2/B3 remain gated. **Current checkpoint (2026-10-25):** Source, target, and independent-group validity still block model selection. The SEN12TS temporal-reference question completed its frozen 43-tile pilot and 38-tile second-rank supplement on external train/development data; schema and mask-coverage gates passed, but component variation and positive normalized-residual correlations prevent treating its temporal power mean as clean/independent truth. Stop further sampling for that question. The CDSE four-anchor graph merged all 29 candidate products into one component, so that pool cannot form a group-disjoint split. No model, selection, or B1/B2/B3 gate changed. **CDSE candidate asset-metadata and protocol review (2026-10-25):** A frozen 80-item inventory returned 45 S1 GRD and 35 S2 L2A Item records, all HTTP 200. The Sentinel legal notice and CDSE terms clear the general open-data rights question with attribution/modified-data wording; authenticated asset access was not tested. S1 metadata advertises VV/VH measurement COGs and per-polarization calibration/noise XML; S2 metadata advertises 10 m B02/B03/B04 reflectance, SCL, and product metadata. No asset URL was stored or followed, and no calibration/noise/product XML, measurement raster, SCL pixel, or image was fetched. A separate deterministic metadata-only reanalysis found at least one 3+-datatake same-platform/orbit-direction/relative-orbit S1 group at each of 12 surveyed AOIs, over 47–59 days; this is reference feasibility only, not proof of temporal stability or a clean target. Mixed S1 product-type strings need harmonization review. See [D-2026-10-25-104](DECISION_LOG.md#d-2026-10-25-104--bound-cdse-asset-metadata-and-repeat-stack-evidence), the [inventory report](../../experiments/data_audits/cdse_candidate_asset_inventory_20261025_v1/report.md), and the [repeat summary](../../experiments/data_audits/cdse_candidate_asset_inventory_20261025_v1/s1_repeat_summary.json).

**CDSE sidecar access path (2026-10-25):** The first selected S1 Item metadata request succeeded, but its calibration/noise asset hrefs use `s3://eodata`. CDSE's official S3 and OData documentation requires registered-account credentials for product access; the process has no AWS/CDSE credential variables and no S3 CLI/SDK. Zero sidecar XML requests were made. The five-item/11-sidecar sample is frozen but blocked pending secure credentials. See [D-2026-10-25-105](DECISION_LOG.md#d-2026-10-25-105--pause-cdse-sidecar-fetch-until-authenticated-access-is-configured) and the [access-path audit](../../experiments/data_audits/cdse_sidecar_schema_review_20261025_v1/report.md).

**CDSE metadata route update (2026-10-25):** Direct STAC v1 metadata searches now work beyond the prior web-reader limitation. The focused Berlin screen returned complete November–December pages (41 S1 GRD, 72 S2 L2A) and one selected same-day S1C/S2C pair with a 6.602 h gap, 0.09% reported cloud cover, and an approximate 2.56 km shared-footprint probe. A frozen four-anchor extension found temporally close, low-cloud candidates at Hamburg, Berlin, Frankfurt, and Munich, but also found S1 and S2 datatake IDs shared across city anchors. Therefore, city labels do not define independent groups. No assets/pixels were downloaded or accessed; there is still no adopted dataset, group-disjoint split, calibrated SAR target, temporal-independence evidence, or pixel-alignment result. See [D-2026-10-25-100](DECISION_LOG.md#d-2026-10-25-100--advance-cdse-to-live-metadata-candidate-screen), [D-2026-10-25-101](DECISION_LOG.md#d-2026-10-25-101--reject-city-anchors-as-source-groups), the [focused audit](../../experiments/data_audits/cdse_stac_pairing_live_20261025_v1/report.md), and the [four-anchor audit](../../experiments/data_audits/cdse_stac_multi_aoi_metadata_20261025_v1/report.md).

**CDSE acquisition/footprint graph (2026-10-25):** The 19 frozen S1/S2 pair records yielded 17 S1 and 12 S2 item nodes. All 29 item geometries were valid Polygons and every exact Item metadata request returned HTTP 200. There are 21 pairing/shared-datatake edges and 141 positive-area product-footprint overlaps. All four anchors merge into one component at intersection thresholds from 1 m² through 1,000,000 m²; pairing/shared-datatake edges alone give nine components. This rejects the current four-anchor product pool as a group-disjoint train/development cohort under the frozen product-footprint rule, but does not reject CDSE for different AOIs. See [D-2026-10-25-102](DECISION_LOG.md#d-2026-10-25-102--reject-the-four-anchor-cdse-pool-as-a-group-disjoint-cohort) and the [graph audit](../../experiments/data_audits/cdse_acquisition_footprint_graph_20261025_v1/report.md).

**Immediate next action:** Keep the fixed local numeric-2k validation and B1F inner-dev partitions out of model selection because their exact target overlaps are documented. The separate `sen12_scene_dev1800_v2` cohort is source-folder-disjoint under its frozen allocation and declared overlap checks, but supports only a fall, single-ROI-season development claim. Its completed three-seed source-aligned B1 screen returned `not_promoted`: mean paired ΔPSNR was +0.001617 dB, below the frozen +0.05 dB threshold, and mean ΔSSIM was −0.000892. The new group summary found positive mean ΔPSNR in 10/15 folders and negative mean ΔSSIM in all 15. Stay at B1; B2/B3 remain gated. Next investigate source/target and fine-registration limits or a new evidence-backed cohort; do not repeat this B1 run or access test data. Cumulative flags remain `test_set_accessed=true`, `test_set_used=false`.

Amplitude-mode squared multipliers match unit-mean Gamma power moments while the amplitude multiplier mean is below one, as expected. This validates implementation moments only; the normalized-PNG representation domain remains non-physical without calibration evidence. No image, model, checkpoint, or test payload was accessed, and no gate changed. **Conditional multitemporal-target dependence derivation (2026-10-25):** Under a static-reflectivity, independent-speckle model, if a noisy acquisition is included in an `N`-image reference mean, the conditional correlation between its speckle residual and the reference's residual is `1/√N` (about 0.447 to 0.200 for the 5–25-look stacks); it is zero when the input is excluded and the remaining acquisitions are independent. This is a model-based derivation, not a measurement of the 2024 or 2026 release. The 2024 Toronto code confirms same-input inclusion for that release; stack membership remains unknown for the 2026 global dataset. No data, experiment, or gate changed. See [literature review](literature_review.md#external-multitemporal-sar-reference-candidate-2026), [risk register](RESEARCH_RISKS.md), and [D-2026-10-25-10](DECISION_LOG.md#d-2026-10-25-10--quantify-input-target-dependence-under-an-idealized-stack-model).

## Status in one sentence

No optical-guided method has passed its declared promotion gate. Historical B1/B1R/B1F and Phase-1 R/S/T results remain unchanged and non-promoting; the local numeric 2k partitions remain unsuitable for independent model selection because of exact source/target overlap. The current-source prospective B1 v2 matrix completed all six runs and its frozen gate returned `not_promoted`: mean paired ΔPSNR was `+0.001617 dB` (below `+0.05 dB`) and mean ΔSSIM was `−0.000892` (below zero), although all three seed ΔPSNR values were positive. B2/B3 remain gated. This is evidence about the tested C2 configuration on the fall-only 15-source-group development cohort, not proof that optical information is intrinsically useless or generalizes across scenes. Fine SAR–optical co-registration, acquisition metadata, and local upstream lineage remain unresolved. The six runs and aggregate retain false test-access/use flags; no test imagery, arrays, or metric files were read for those runs. A separate text-level exposure of legacy pilot results is recorded below and did not change historical run flags. Rebuildable training inputs and per-epoch artifacts were removed after sealing; see the run's `artifact_pruning_audit.json`.

## Current research stage

**ProSR full-text audit (2026-10-24):** ProSR adds discrete-token, semantic-prototype-guided SAR ×4 SR prior art using Umbra SLC spectral-crop inputs. The source-disjoint Umbra protocol reports train/validation but no separate SR test; stored amplitude is per-scene normalized, clipped, and quantized, and model code is unavailable. The paper's scatterer-merging concern is a method-specific reason to inspect peak structure, not evidence for optical-guided joint restoration or a universal diffusion failure. The curated dataset card and Umbra source-imagery pages show different reuse licenses. No local experiment or gate changed. ## Source-aligned B1 v2 rerun (2026-10-23)

The fresh root is `experiments/sen12_scene_dev1800_b1_revalidation_20261023_source_aligned_v1/`. Its `source_hashes.json` binds 26 code/protocol/audit dependencies, including `scripts/training_resume.py`. All 26 matched the worktree when the run was sealed; the later D-2026-10-23-46 interface maintenance changed exactly two files in the current tree, while the run-bound hashes remain untouched. The 2026-10-22 root remains untouched with four sealed results and an unevaluated gate; those results are excluded because four source hashes differ. The new six-run matrix completed under the frozen protocol: SAR-only best epochs were 14/15/19 and C2 best epochs were 1/1/1. The C2 run is not promoted: paired mean ΔPSNR `+0.001617 dB` (95% source-group bootstrap CI `[+0.000075, +0.003180]`), mean ΔSSIM `−0.000892`; the frozen thresholds are `+0.05 dB` and `0`, respectively. All three seed ΔPSNR values were positive; other guardrails passed. The cohort is fall-only across 15 validation source folders, nominally source-matched with fine co-registration unverified, and uses normalized PNG-derived noisy pseudo-targets. No test data or metrics were accessed; B2/B3 were not run. The completed run retained canonical validation results, best/last checkpoints, full training summaries, configs, commands, manifests, environment/source/input audits, aggregate outputs, and `artifact_pruning_audit.json`; 122 per-epoch/duplicate run files and the 16,218-file, 2.795 GB temporary input workspace were removed (seed0 pruning is also recorded there). The earlier `meshgrid` future-compatibility warning remains in the retained training logs.

**Read-only source-group diagnostic (2026-10-23):** Recomputed the 15×3 group/seed means from the canonical 600-row paired validation CSV and matched the retained `per_group_summary.csv` to within `1e-12`. Mean ΔSSIM was negative in all 45 group/seed cells and in all 15 groups after averaging the three seeds. Positive group-mean ΔPSNR counts were 7/15, 9/15, and 12/15 for seeds 0, 1, and 2; the three-seed group means ranged from `−0.003622` to `+0.007420 dB`. This is a descriptive proxy-target pattern: it shows the observed SSIM decline is not concentrated in one source folder, but does not identify a cause or establish clean-SAR structural harm. Test flags remain false; this diagnostic did not change the gate or select a subgroup.

**Read-only bootstrap rederivation (2026-10-23):** An independent implementation reproduced all eight B1 v2 source-group bootstrap means, 95% intervals, and positive-draw probabilities from the canonical 600-row validation CSV; the maximum absolute difference from the retained summary was `2.71e-20`. The unchanged gate remains `not_promoted` because mean ΔPSNR is below `+0.05 dB` and mean ΔSSIM is below zero; the intervals are not gate inputs. Uncertainty is conditional on three fixed training seeds and 15 source groups from one fall ROI-season. No images or test measurements were read. See the [rederivation audit](../../experiments/diagnostics/b1_v2_source_group_bootstrap_rederivation_20261023_v1/report.md).

**Read-only C2 loss/selection diagnostic (2026-10-23):** All three sealed configs set Charbonnier reconstruction weight to `1.0` and use a train-only calibrated high-frequency residual weight (`0.739918`, `0.808352`, and `0.771583` for seeds 0/1/2); direct SSIM, edge, guard, and other optional loss weights are zero, with the SwinIR backbone frozen for all 10 epochs. Each config's residual weight matches its calibration record. Recomputed `validation_selection_score` from logged PSNR, SSIM, edge ratio, and TBR ratio using the checked `strict_score` implementation; all three runs match the recorded score and select epoch 1. From epoch 1 to 10, validation total loss decreases in each seed while SSIM and selection score decrease and residual-contribution RMS rises. This is consistent with a possible training-objective/selection-metric tension, but does not identify its cause or prove that adding an SSIM term would help. No additional training or gate change was made.

**Read-only per-image direction audit (2026-10-23):** Reanalyzed the sealed 600-row B1 v2 paired validation CSV after verifying its aggregate-manifest hash and false test flags. Averaging the three fixed-seed deltas by identity, PSNR improved on 129/200 identities (106 in all three seeds; 45 worsened in all three), while SSIM worsened on 192/200. Edge-strength ratio error increased on all 200 identities in each seed. The top 40 identities contributed 61.4% of the sum of positive image-mean PSNR deltas. This makes the small PSNR mean broader than a handful of cases but concentrated, alongside consistent structure-proxy regression. It is descriptive only: identities are nested in 15 source groups, and the noisy pseudo-target does not establish clean-SAR utility or a cause. The unchanged B1 gate remains `not_promoted`; no training, inference, selection, subgroup choice, or test access occurred. See the [distribution audit](../../experiments/diagnostics/b1_v2_per_image_heterogeneity_20261023_v1/report.md).

**Read-only C2 objective/code audit (2026-10-23):** The only active C2 losses are reconstruction and a train-calibrated high-frequency residual penalty; the backbone remains frozen for all ten epochs. Across all three seeds, validation total loss falls from epoch 1 to 10 while SSIM and strict selection score fall and residual-contribution RMS rises. This supports a possible objective/selection-metric tension, not a proven cause or a proposed same-cohort retune. The run-bound loss source hash matches current code. The model and trainer hashes differ from current files because of the later D-2026-10-23-46 interface maintenance; the two mismatches are recorded without changing historical manifests. See the [audit](../../experiments/diagnostics/b1_v2_c2_objective_code_audit_20261023_v1/report.md) and [access manifest](../../experiments/diagnostics/b1_v2_c2_objective_code_audit_20261023_v1/access_manifest.json).

## B1 v2 C2 failure-cause synthesis (2026-10-23)

The gate failure is explained by the two frozen thresholds that failed: mean ΔPSNR `+0.001617 dB` is below `+0.05 dB`, and mean ΔSSIM `−0.000892` is below zero; all other registered guardrails pass. PSNR improved on 129/200 image means, while SSIM worsened on 192/200 and edge-strength ratio error worsened on all 200 in each seed. The training objective/selection mismatch is plausible but not causal; geometry/acquisition, pseudo-target/degradation, and frozen-backbone capacity explanations remain unresolved. This is validation-proxy evidence from the single fall-only cohort, not clean-SAR evidence. Keep B1 `not_promoted`, do not retune this cohort or enter B2/B3. See the [synthesis audit](../../experiments/diagnostics/b1_v2_c2_root_cause_synthesis_20261023_v1/report.md).

**Data provenance scope:** For the separate 1,800-pair prospective cohort, all selected SAR/optical PNGs were matched to official SEN1-2 source pixels and the second-level `s1_i`/`s2_i` folders identify source scenes; the 15/15 source-folder split is verified. The official supporting document describes 8-bit sigma-nought dB SAR and optical bands 4/3/2, but does not supply per-pair acquisition times or residual registration measurements. This resolves source-folder pedigree for the 1,800 cohort only. The original numeric 2k subset still has unresolved upstream cache/parent-scene lineage. See the [cohort audit](../../experiments/data_audits/sen12_scene_dev1800_v2_auth_20261021_v1/report.md) and [official supporting document](../../experiments/data_audits/sen1_2_upstream_hash_crosswalk_20261019_v1/SupportingDocument.txt).

## SEN1-2 acquisition timing and mosaic follow-up (2026-10-23)

The full 2018 SEN1-2 paper confirms that the fall cohort comes from season-filtered Sentinel-1 IW/VV and Sentinel-2 collections mosaicked per ROI; it documents the 2017 fall window but gives no pairwise date rule, maximum time gap, product IDs, or measured residual registration. The official TUM supporting document identifies the `s1_i`/`s2_i` scene folders and 8-bit representations, but not those product-level fields. The TUM record was rate-limited (HTTP 429) during this recheck, and a bounded author-code search found no public generator or date crosswalk. These findings are limited to the examined sources. An `ImageCollection.mosaic()` may combine pixels from multiple collection members, but the actual per-pixel temporal mixture is unknown. See the [source audit](../../experiments/literature_audits/sen12_pair_timing_registration_20261023_v1/report.md).

The B1 v2 cohort is therefore source-pixel authenticated and source-folder-disjoint, but its optical condition is not established as simultaneous or subpixel-aligned. Keep the result conditional on same-season mosaics and nominal georegistration. The gate remains `not_promoted`; B2/B3 remain gated.

**Third-party SIFT-label probe (2026-10-23):** The public SEN1-2 patch-matching training list contains exact names for 124/200 frozen validation pairs (62%). All 219 label-1 coordinate pairs have zero SAR-to-optical integer offset; the 215 label-0 examples have much larger offsets. A follow-up checked the README's code link: it resolves to the author's RRL-Net training/testing repository, which describes prepared dataset archives but not SIFT-label generation; the dataset repository lists no generation script. Independent surveying remains undocumented, and the full FIL-Net article method was not reviewed. Treat this as evidence about the derivative's labels, not as a measured registration residual. It does not resolve fine alignment or acquisition timing; no test-list contents or images were accessed. See the [audit](../../experiments/data_audits/sen12_sift_tiepoint_probe_20261023_v1/report.md).

## SEN12TP conditional candidate audit (2026-10-23)

The primary article and Zenodo record describe 2,319 Sentinel-1/2 pairs across 1,236 areas, with per-scene timestamps and ROI IDs, spatial grouping, and at most 12 hours between acquisitions (usually about six). The author dataloader expects ROI-directory splits, but neither the release's published 90/10 train/test archive layout nor the article's 80/10/10 split has been mapped to exact metadata memberships; repeated dates must be grouped by `roi_id`. Zenodo's visible license field remains blank, while OpenAIRE's merged entry displays a version-unspecified `CC BY` label; Zenodo's generic developer and Help docs give different default-license descriptions. Exact archive and curated-metadata rights therefore remain unresolved. Official component-source terms suggest a feasible attributed reuse route, but do not establish rights for the compiled archive or curated metadata. The Zenodo metadata API/export route was inaccessible and its metadata JSON preview returned “Cannot preview file”; neither metadata contents nor archives were accessed. The public file list includes five train archives although its prose says four, plus a test archive; the dataset has no clean SAR restoration target. Keep it as a conditional provenance/timing source only. A search result surfaced one paper-reported aggregate test-metric text during the split-method check; it was not used for candidate evaluation. No dataset test identity, image, or raw measurement file and no local project test data/metrics were accessed. See the [audit](../../experiments/literature_audits/sen12tp_candidate_20261023_v1/report.md) and its [access manifest](../../experiments/literature_audits/sen12tp_candidate_20261023_v1/access_manifest.json).

## Prospective B1 run-interface preflight (2026-10-21)

Source review of `scripts/train_swinir_joint.py`, `scripts/train_mmcanet_v2.py`, and `scripts/aggregate_source_group_b1.py` found an integration gap before any model run. The trainers write a root `manifest.json`, epoch metrics under `validation_per_image/epoch_####.csv`, and checkpoints. The aggregator contract instead requires each run's `protocol_id`, role, architecture, fixed-input `files.csv` hashes, explicit `candidate_test_enumerated=false`, and (for the optical run) stage/candidate, plus `validation/manifest.json` and `validation/per_image.csv` bound to the selected best epoch. The current trainer outputs do not supply this contract and cannot be passed directly to the aggregator. The protocol's `strict_score` formula is implemented identically in `OGSRN/metrics.py`, and the listed training parameters/loss terms are expressible by the trainers. No code or run output was changed in this read-only preflight. The required bridge was subsequently implemented and synthetically verified below.

## Prospective B1 run-sealing implementation (2026-10-21; synthetic fixtures only)

Implemented [`scripts/seal_source_group_b1_run.py`](../../scripts/seal_source_group_b1_run.py) to convert one completed native SwinIRJoint or MMCANetV2 run into the frozen source-group aggregator contract. It checks the native config hash and protocol settings, explicit false test flags, CUDA run metadata, fixed-input manifest/file-list/split/source-protocol hashes and canonical identities, the training log's strict-score best epoch, checkpoint path/hash, and every required finite best-epoch validation metric in canonical order. For the optical candidate it also verifies the per-seed calibration's train-manifest hash, deterministic first train identities, same-seed SAR-only checkpoint hash, gradient formula, and equality to the configured residual weight. It writes the selected epoch into `validation/per_image.csv`, its validation manifest, and the aggregator-required root fields; existing canonical output paths fail closed. It reads manifests, CSVs, split metadata, calibration records, and checkpoint bytes for hashing only; no image/tensor payloads are opened.

The new tests cover both roles, candidate-calibration binding, rejection paths, and a six-run synthetic handoff through `aggregate_source_group_b1.py`: 21 focused tests pass and the two Python files compile. The full suite reports 232 passed / 2 failed. The failures are `test_fixed_protocol_train_validation_contract`, which expects the deliberately pruned `fixed_train_dev2k/lr_sar/8000.npy`, and `test_final_b1_artifacts_are_fail_closed`, whose historical source-hash manifest differs from four existing untracked files (`scripts/calibrate_phase_b1_residual_weight.py`, `scripts/train_mmcanet_v2.py`, `MMCANetV2/model.py`, and `JointJDSR/model.py`). Neither failure was changed here. No formal model run, inference, metric selection, test image/measurement access, or gate change occurred. The full suite did run the existing split-integrity test `test_ogsrn_protocol.py::test_fixed_ogsrn_splits_are_complete_and_disjoint`, which reads only `fixed_test_dev2k` manifest/split/file-name metadata and guards test-array loading with a patched `np.load`.
